Null values (empty, or `NA`,\n  `N/A`, `NULL` ignoring case) are not listed.\n- A column with no non-null values writes only the header.\n- At most the `--top` limit of rows is written; the default limit is 5 (the limit itself is owned by\n  FEATURE-FrequencyTop.md).\n- Output is CSV with `\\n` line endings, written with `encoding/csv`, and only after the whole file\n  has been read and validated.\n- Success exits 0.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC freq-department ===\nIntent: Frequencies for a text column are counted, ordered by count then byte value, and parsed back as CSV.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/employees.csv\")\ncol = \"department\"\ntop = 5\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\nordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:top]]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[0] == [\"value\", \"count\"], got\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\n=== END AC freq-department ===\n\n=== AC freq-tie-order ===\nIntent: Values with equal counts are ordered ascending by byte value and the limit is honored.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nfor fname, col, top in ((\"orders.csv\", \"customer\", 5), (\"employees.csv\", \"manager_id\", 10)):\n    path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(\"sources\", fname))\n    with open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n        rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\n    header = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\n    idx = header.index(col)\n    vals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\n    vals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\n    ordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\n    want = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:top]]\n\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, \"--top\", str(top), path)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n    got = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert got[1:] == want, (fname, got[1:], want)\n    listed = {r[0] for r in got[1:]}\n    assert \"\" not in listed and \"NA\" not in listed\n=== END AC freq-trims-and-skips-nulls ===\n\n=== AC freq-all-null-column ===\nIntent: A column with no non-null values writes only the header and exits 0.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; every value comes from the file examined.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` or any supplied data file.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-FrequencyTop.md ===\n# FEATURE: Frequency Limit\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Limits frequency output with --top, defaulting to 5 and accepting a positive integer only. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Frequency.md, FEATURE-UsageErrors.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | csvstat --freq <name> --top <n> <file> |\n| Consumes    | value,count output |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`--top <n>` bounds how many rows `--freq` writes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Default limit is 5 when `--top` is absent.\n- `--top` accepts a positive integer only. Zero, negative numbers, non-numeric text, and an empty\n  value are usage errors (exit 2, nothing on standard output).\n- When fewer distinct non-null values exist than the limit, all of them are written.\n- `--top` without `--freq` is a usage error (exit 2, nothing on standard output).\n- A very large positive integer that fits no machine int is accepted as unbounded rather than\n  rejected.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC top-default-is-five ===\nIntent: Without --top, a column with many distinct values lists exactly five rows.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/orders.csv\")\ncol = \"order_date\"\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\nordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\ndefault_limit = 5\nassert len(ordered) > default_limit\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:default_limit]]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\n=== END AC top-default-is-five ===\n\n=== AC top-limits-rows ===\nIntent: An explicit --top n writes at most n rows, matching the ordered counts derived from the file.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/employees.csv\")\ncol = \"department\"\ntop = 100\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\ncounts = Counter(vals)\nordered = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, \"--top\", str(top), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\nassert len(got) - 1 == len(counts)\n=== END AC top-larger-than-distinct ===\n\n=== AC top-rejects-non-positive ===\nIntent: A --top that is not a positive integer exits 2 with nothing on standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; Usage errors are decided before the file is opened, so\na usage error for a missing file path never becomes exit 1. `--help` is not a usage error and is\nowned by FEATURE-CliParsing.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-missing-or-extra-file ===\nIntent: No file, or more than one file, exits 2 with empty standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Frequency.md ===\n# FEATURE: Frequency Listing\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Lists the most frequent non-null values of one named column with --freq. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-ProfileCounts.md, FEATURE-CliParsing.md |\n| Provides    | value,count output |\n| Consumes    | csvstat --freq <name> <file> |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`csvstat --freq <name> <file>` writes a two-column CSV of the distinct non-null values of the named\ncolumn and how often each occurs, instead of the profile.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header line is `value,count`.\n- One row per distinct non-null value, ordered by count descending, then by value ascending by byte\n  value.\n- Values are trimmed before counting and compared as exact strings. Null values (empty, or `NA`,\n  `N/A`, `NULL` ignoring case) are not listed.\n- A column with no non-null values writes only the header.\n- At most the `--top` limit of rows is written; the default limit is 5 (the limit itself is owned by\n  FEATURE-FrequencyTop.md).\n- Output is CSV with `\\n` line endings, written with `encoding/csv`, and only after the whole file\n  has been read and validated.\n- Success exits 0.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC freq-department ===\nIntent: Frequencies for a text column are counted, ordered by count then byte value, and parsed back as CSV.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/employees.csv\")\ncol = \"department\"\ntop = 5\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\nordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:top]]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[0] == [\"value\", \"count\"], got\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\n=== END AC freq-department ===\n\n=== AC freq-tie-order ===\nIntent: Values with equal counts are ordered ascending by byte value and the limit is honored.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/sensors.csv\")\ncol = \"sensor\"\ntop = 2\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\nordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:top]]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, \"--top\", str(top), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\nassert len(got) == top + 1\n=== END AC freq-tie-order ===\n\n=== AC freq-trims-and-skips-nulls ===\nIntent: Padded values are trimmed before counting and null spellings (blank, NA) are not listed.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nfor fname, col, top in ((\"orders.csv\", \"customer\", 5), (\"employees.csv\", \"manager_id\", 10)):\n    path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(\"sources\", fname))\n    with open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n        rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\n    header = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\n    idx = header.index(col)\n    vals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\n    vals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\n    ordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\n    want = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:top]]\n\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, \"--top\", str(top), path)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n    got = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert got[1:] == want, (fname, got[1:], want)\n    listed = {r[0] for r in got[1:]}\n    assert \"\" not in listed and \"NA\" not in listed\n=== END AC freq-trims-and-skips-nulls ===\n\n=== AC freq-all-null-column ===\nIntent: A column with no non-null values writes only the header and exits 0.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; Zero, negative numbers, non-numeric text, and an empty\n  value are usage errors (exit 2, nothing on standard output).\n- When fewer distinct non-null values exist than the limit, all of them are written.\n- `--top` without `--freq` is a usage error (exit 2, nothing on standard output).\n- A very large positive integer that fits no machine int is accepted as unbounded rather than\n  rejected.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC top-default-is-five ===\nIntent: Without --top, a column with many distinct values lists exactly five rows.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/orders.csv\")\ncol = \"order_date\"\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\nordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\ndefault_limit = 5\nassert len(ordered) > default_limit\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:default_limit]]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\n=== END AC top-default-is-five ===\n\n=== AC top-limits-rows ===\nIntent: An explicit --top n writes at most n rows, matching the ordered counts derived from the file.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/employees.csv\")\ncol = \"manager_id\"\ntop = 3\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\nordered = sorted(Counter(vals).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered[:top]]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, \"--top\", str(top), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\nassert len(got) - 1 <= top\n=== END AC top-limits-rows ===\n\n=== AC top-larger-than-distinct ===\nIntent: A limit above the distinct count lists every distinct non-null value.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(\"sources/employees.csv\")\ncol = \"department\"\ntop = 100\nwith open(path, newline=\"\") as fh:\n    rows = list(csv.reader(fh))\nheader = [h.strip() for h in rows[0]]\nidx = header.index(col)\nvals = [r[idx].strip() for r in rows[1:]]\nvals = [v for v in vals if v != \"\" and v.upper() not in (\"NA\", \"N/A\", \"NULL\")]\ncounts = Counter(vals)\nordered = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0].encode()))\nwant = [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ordered]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", col, \"--top\", str(top), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\ngot = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert got[1:] == want, (got[1:], want)\nassert len(got) - 1 == len(counts)\n=== END AC top-larger-than-distinct ===\n\n=== AC top-rejects-non-positive ===\nIntent: A --top that is not a positive integer exits 2 with nothing on standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=drydock_go; A\n  failure of either exits non-zero; a vet failure does not let the test run mask it.\n- It uses whatever `go` is on `PATH` and never a vendored toolchain.\n- The Go test suite it runs covers each type, each statistic, null handling, rounding, each output\n  form, and each exit code, using files the tests write themselves (not the files under `sources/`).\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC testrunner-syntax ===\nIntent: bin/test.sh exists and passes a POSIX syntax check.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=sh; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\n\nassert os.path.isfile(\"bin/test.sh\")\nresult = subprocess.run([\"sh\", \"-n\", \"bin/test.sh\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\n=== END AC testrunner-syntax ===\n\n=== AC testrunner-runs-vet-and-test ===\nIntent: With a passing toolchain stub, bin/test.sh exits zero and invokes both go vet and go test.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=sh; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    log = os.path.join(tmp, \"calls.log\")\n    stub = os.path.join(tmp, \"go\")\n    with open(stub, \"w\") as fh:\n        fh.write('#!/bin/sh\\necho \"$1\" >> \"$GO_LOG\"\\n[ \"$1\" = \"$FAIL_ON\" ] && exit 1\\nexit 0\\n')\n    os.chmod(stub, 0o755)\n    env = {**os.environ, \"PATH\": tmp + os.pathsep + os.environ[\"PATH\"], \"GO_LOG\": log, \"FAIL_ON\": \"none\"}\n    result = subprocess.run([\"sh\", \"bin/test.sh\"], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)\n    print(result.stdout)\n    print(result.stderr)\n    assert result.returncode == 0\n    with open(log) as fh:\n        calls = fh.read().split()\n    assert \"vet\" in calls, calls\n    assert \"test\" in calls, calls\n=== END AC testrunner-runs-vet-and-test ===\n\n=== AC testrunner-vet-failure ===\nIntent: When go vet fails, bin/test.sh exits non-zero.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=sh; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nscript = os.path.abspath(\"bin/test.sh\")\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    log = os.path.join(tmp, \"calls.log\")\n    stub = os.path.join(tmp, \"go\")\n    with open(stub, \"w\") as fh:\n        fh.write('#!/bin/sh\\necho \"$1\" >> \"$GO_LOG\"\\n[ \"$1\" = \"$FAIL_ON\" ] && exit 1\\nexit 0\\n')\n    os.chmod(stub, 0o755)\n    env = {**os.environ, \"PATH\": tmp + os.pathsep + os.environ[\"PATH\"], \"GO_LOG\": log, \"FAIL_ON\": \"none\"}\n    result = subprocess.run([\"sh\", script], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env, cwd=tmp)\n    print(result.stdout)\n    print(result.stderr)\n    assert result.returncode == 0\n    with open(log) as fh:\n        calls = fh.read().split()\n    assert \"vet\" in calls and \"test\" in calls, calls\n=== END AC testrunner-works-from-other-directory ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `bin/test.sh` is POSIX sh and never relies on bash.\n- It does not run `sources/run.sh`;
